\displaystyle+\frac{i}{5}T_{w_{t}}T_{\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\bar{R}}T_{J^{\frac{3}{2}}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-2}R+G+\text{quartic and higher terms}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}T_{w}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}T_{(1-\bar{Y})^{2}}R+\frac{1}{2}T_{w}T_{\bar{R}}T_{(1-Y)^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}+G,

\displaystyle T_{D_{t}}r^{1,2}_{c}-i\mathcal{L}_{para}w^{1,2}_{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-3iT_{w}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\mathbf{W}+\frac{1}{2}T_{w}T_{\bar{R}}T_{1-Y}R_{\alpha}-\frac{1}{5}T_{w_{t}}T_{\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\bar{R}}T_{(1-Y)^{2}(1+\bar{\mathbf{W}})}\partial_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle+T_{w_{t}}\frac{1}{2}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}}T_{1-\bar{Y}}R+K+\text{quartic and higher terms}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-3iT_{w}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\mathbf{W}+\frac{1}{2}T_{w}T_{\bar{R}}T_{1-Y}R_{\alpha}+K.

As a consequence of above computations, the cubic normal form variables
(w^{1}_{c},r^{1}_{c}):=(w^{1,1}_{c},r^{1,1}_{c})+(w^{1,2}_{c},r^{1,2}_{c})
solve the system

\begin{cases}T_{D_{t}}w^{1}_{c}+T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}r^{1}_{c}+T_{T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}}w^{1}_{c}=-\mathcal{G}_{1}^{[3]}+G,\\
T_{D_{t}}r^{1}_{c}-i\mathcal{L}_{para}w^{1}_{c}=-\mathcal{K}_{1}^{[3]}+K.\end{cases}

To conclude this subsection, by choosing normal form variables
(w_{NF}^{1},r_{NF}^{1})
as in (
3.11
), they satisfy (
3.9
) and solve the system (
3.10
).

4.
Energy estimate for the hydroelastic waves

In this section, we derive the modified energy estimate for the differentiated two-dimensional hydroelastic wave system (
1.7
).
Rather than constructing the modified energy
E_{s}(\mathbf{W},R)
directly, we proceed in several steps.
First, we rewrite (
1.7
) in the paradifferential form in Section
4.1
, moving non-perturbative balanced source terms to the right-hand side.
Then, in Section
4.2
, we construct balanced normal form variables
(\mathbf{W}_{NF},R_{NF})
so that the paradifferential equations of
(\mathbf{W}_{NF},R_{NF})
no longer have balanced perturbative source terms.
Next, we demonstrate how to reduce Theorem
1.1
to the modified energy estimate of the homogeneous paradifferential system (
3.1
) in Section
4.3
.
Finally, in Section
4.4
, we prove the modified energy estimate of (
3.1
) by partially exploiting Proposition
3.2
, which ultimately yields the modified energy estimate for
(\mathbf{W},R)
.

Throughout this section, we denote by
(G,K)
perturbative source terms satisfying

(4.1)

\|(G,K)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}}\lesssim_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}{\mathcal{A}_{\sharp,\frac{7}{4}}^{2}}\|(\mathbf{W},R)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}},\quad s>0,

or
(G_{s},K_{s})
that satisfy (
4.10
).

4.1.
Paradifferential reduction of hydroelastic waves

First, we rewrite the differentiated two-dimensional hydroelastic wave system (
1.7
) as a system of paradifferential equations, placing balanced paraproducts and perturbative terms on the right-hand side.

Recall that
(\mathbf{W},R)
solve the system

\begin{cases}&D_{t}\mathbf{W}+(1-\bar{Y})(1+\mathbf{W})R_{\alpha}=(1+\mathbf{W})M,\\
&D_{t}R+ia(1-Y)-i(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}\partial_{\alpha}\left\{J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left[J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)\right)\right]\right\}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}i(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}\partial_{\alpha}\left[\mathbf{W}^{3}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}-3\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\right]\\
&=-i(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}\partial_{\alpha}\left\{J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left[J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\right)\right]\right\}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}i(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}\partial_{\alpha}\left[\bar{\mathbf{W}}^{3}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})^{3}-3\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\right].\end{cases}

Following Section 6 in
[
3
]
,
\mathbf{W}
solves the paradifferential equation

(4.2)

\displaystyle T_{D_{t}}\mathbf{W}+T_{b_{\alpha}}\mathbf{W}+\partial_{\alpha}T_{1-\bar{Y}}T_{1+\mathbf{W}}R

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}\Pi(\mathbf{W},R)-T_{1-Y}\partial_{\alpha}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{R},\mathbf{W})-T_{(1-\bar{Y})^{2}(1+\mathbf{W})}\partial_{\alpha}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},R)+G.

For the first two terms in the second equation of the hydroelastic waves, we apply the projection
{\mathbf{P}}
and write

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}D_{t}R+{\mathbf{P}}(ia(1-Y))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{D_{t}}R+T_{R_{\alpha}}{\mathbf{P}}b+{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(R_{\alpha},b)+iT_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}a-iT_{a}Y-i{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(a,Y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{D_{t}}R+T_{R_{\alpha}}T_{1-\bar{Y}}R+{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(R_{\alpha},T_{1-\bar{Y}}R+T_{1-Y}\bar{R})+T_{1-Y}T_{\bar{R}_{\alpha}}R+T_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{R}_{\alpha},R)+K

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{D_{t}}R+T_{b_{\alpha}}R+T_{1-\bar{Y}}\Pi(R_{\alpha},R)+T_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(R_{\alpha},\bar{R})+T_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{R}_{\alpha},R)+K.

It remains to consider the elastic terms in the equation of
R
.
For a fixed real constant
\beta
,

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}J^{-\beta}=

\displaystyle-\beta J^{-\beta}(1-Y)\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-\beta J^{-\beta}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\quad\partial_{\alpha}Y=(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha},

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\beta}(1-Y))=

\displaystyle-(\beta+1)J^{-\beta}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-\beta J^{-\beta-1}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},

\displaystyle\partial^{2}_{\alpha}(J^{-\beta}(1-Y))=

\displaystyle-(\beta+1)J^{-\beta}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\beta J^{-\beta-1}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+(\beta+2)(\beta+1)J^{-\beta}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}+2\beta(\beta+1)J^{-\beta-1}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\beta(\beta+1)J^{-\beta-1}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}.

From these, we derive the following identities:

\displaystyle J^{-1}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y))+J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-1}(1-Y))+\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-6J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-3J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},

\displaystyle J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{9}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle+J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}+\frac{5}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}|^{2},

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-1}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{21}{4}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}+\frac{7}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}|^{2},

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-1}(1-Y)))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+7J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}+\frac{11}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}|^{2}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)))+\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-1}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)))+\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-1}(1-Y)))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-5J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-2J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{67}{4}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{19}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}+\frac{23}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}|^{2}.

Direct computation yields

\displaystyle J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{9}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle+J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}+\frac{5}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}|^{2},

we then have

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{1}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}+\frac{57}{4}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{13}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{19}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{15}{4}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{81}{4}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{4}\mathbf{W}^{3}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{31}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}-\frac{35}{4}J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}-\frac{7}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}^{3}_{\alpha}.

As a consequence,

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}\left\{J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left[J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+\left(J^{-1}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y))+J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-1}(1-Y))+\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y))\right)\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle+\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)))+\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-1}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)))+\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}(J^{-1}(1-Y)))\right)\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)\right)\right)\right)\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-6J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-3J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle-5J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha\alpha}-2J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}|^{2}+\frac{67}{4}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{19}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{23}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}|^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{3}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}+\frac{57}{4}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{13}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{19}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{15}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+K

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{15}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-3J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{1}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}

\displaystyle-5J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha\alpha}-2J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}|^{2}+31J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{13}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{65}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{15}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{19}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+K.

We derive

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}\left[\mathbf{W}^{3}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}-3\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-6J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-3J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+K.

Therefore, we obtain the expression for quantity
A
defined below

\displaystyle A:=

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}\left\{J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left[J^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}\left(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-Y)\right)\right]\right\}+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\alpha}\left[\mathbf{W}^{3}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}-3\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{15}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-3J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{1}{2}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}

\displaystyle-5J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha\alpha}-2J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}|^{2}+\frac{65}{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{13}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{53}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{9}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{19}{4}J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+K.

Using the notation of
A
, the elastic terms are simply

-i(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}A+i(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}.

We first compute
{\mathbf{P}}A
. We use the paralinearization in Lemma
A.6
to write

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}]

\displaystyle=T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+{\mathbf{P}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}(J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)-1)+{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)-1)

\displaystyle=T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{5}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}-\frac{5}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\bar{\mathbf{W}})+K,

\displaystyle-\frac{15}{2}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}]

\displaystyle=-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{15}{2}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}]

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{15}{2}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle=-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})+K,

\displaystyle-3{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}]

\displaystyle=-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-3T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}{\mathbf{P}}(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha})-3{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle=-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha})+K,

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})+K,

\displaystyle-5{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=-5T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-5T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}{\mathbf{P}}(J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle\quad-5{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle=-10T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-5T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})+K,

\displaystyle-2{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}|^{2}]

\displaystyle=-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})+K,

\displaystyle\frac{65}{2}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=\frac{65}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+K,

\displaystyle\frac{13}{4}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=K,

\displaystyle\frac{53}{4}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=\frac{53}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+K,

\displaystyle\frac{9}{4}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=K,

\displaystyle\frac{19}{4}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=\frac{19}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+K.

Collecting all above terms, we obtain the expression for
PA
:

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}A

\displaystyle=T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-10T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}+\frac{65}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{53}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{19}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-5T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})-\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})+K.

Using the expression of
{\mathbf{P}}A
, we derive the expression for
(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}A
:

\displaystyle(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}A=T_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}A-T_{{\mathbf{P}}A}Y-\Pi({\mathbf{P}}A,Y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}A-T_{{\mathbf{P}}A}T_{(1-Y)^{2}}\mathbf{W}-\Pi({\mathbf{P}}A,T_{(1-Y)^{2}}\mathbf{W})+K

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle-10T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{65}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{4}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{53}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{19}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle-\frac{15}{2}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\mathbf{W})-\frac{3}{2}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}\bar{\mathbf{W}})-\frac{15}{2}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle-3{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha})-\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-5{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle-2{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})-T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}-{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\mathbf{W})+K

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle-10T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{65}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{4}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{53}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{19}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{7}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{7}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})-\frac{15}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-5T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})+K.

Similarly, we compute
{\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}
.

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}]

\displaystyle=T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}{\mathbf{P}}(J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})-1)+{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})-1)

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}-\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W})+K,

\displaystyle-3{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}]

\displaystyle=-3T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}{\mathbf{P}}(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-3{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle=-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})+K,

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{1}{2}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha})+K,

\displaystyle-2{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}|^{2}]

\displaystyle=-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})+K,

\displaystyle\frac{13}{4}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=\frac{13}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+K,

\displaystyle\frac{53}{4}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=K,

\displaystyle\frac{9}{4}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}^{2}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=\frac{9}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}^{2}_{\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+K,

\displaystyle\frac{19}{4}{\mathbf{P}}[J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}]

\displaystyle=K.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}=

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{13}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{9}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}^{2}_{\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})+K.

Using the expression of
{\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}
, we get the equation for
(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}
:

\displaystyle(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}=

\displaystyle T_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}-T_{{\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}}T_{(1-Y)^{2}}\mathbf{W}-\Pi({\mathbf{P}}\bar{A},T_{(1-Y)^{2}}\mathbf{W})+K

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}+\frac{13}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{9}{4}T_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}^{2}_{\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W})-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle-2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})-\frac{1}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})+K.

Combining the expressions for
(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}A
and
(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}
, the elastic terms can be written as:

\displaystyle\text{Elastic terms}=-i(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}A+i(1-Y){\mathbf{P}}\bar{A}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+3iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle+10iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+2iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{65}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{4}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{53}{4}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{19}{4}iT_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}+\frac{1}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})+5iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})+3iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})+2iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}-\frac{1}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle-2iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-3iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}+\frac{13}{4}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{9}{4}iT_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}^{2}_{\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W})-3iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle-2iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})-\frac{1}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})+K

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle+10iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}-\frac{65}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{4}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle-10iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})+5iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})+\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W})+K.

Therefore, by moving balanced and perturbative terms to the right-hand side,
R
solves the paradifferential equation

\displaystyle T_{D_{t}}R+T_{b_{\alpha}}R-iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+10iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle-\frac{65}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{4}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-10iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-T_{1-\bar{Y}}\Pi(R_{\alpha},R)-T_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}\partial_{\alpha}\Pi(\bar{R},R)-\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle-\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-5iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})+\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W})+K.

4.2.
Normal form transformation of the hydroelastic waves

In this section, we demonstrate the existence of balanced normal form corrections
(\tilde{\mathbf{W}},\tilde{R})
capable of removing the non-perturbative balanced source terms in the paradifferential equations for
\mathbf{W}
and
R
.
Specifically, we seek quadratic normal form corrections of balanced type
(\tilde{\mathbf{W}},\tilde{R})
such that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}+T_{1-\bar{Y}}T_{1+\mathbf{W}}\tilde{R}_{\alpha}+\text{cubic and higher terms}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}\Pi(\mathbf{W},R)+T_{1-Y}\partial_{\alpha}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{R},\mathbf{W})+T_{(1-\bar{Y})^{2}(1+\mathbf{W})}\partial_{\alpha}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},R),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\tilde{R}-iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}+\text{cubic and higher terms}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{1-\bar{Y}}\Pi(R_{\alpha},R)+T_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}\partial_{\alpha}\Pi(\bar{R},R)+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})+5iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})+\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}).

We consider balanced quadratic normal form corrections as the sum of the holomorphic type and the mixed type:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{W}}

\displaystyle=T_{1-Y}B^{h}_{bal}(\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})+T_{J^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+\mathbf{W})^{3}}C^{h}_{bal}(R,R)+B^{a}_{bal}(\bar{\mathbf{W}},T_{1-\bar{Y}}\mathbf{W})+C^{a}_{bal}\Big(\bar{R},T_{J^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+\mathbf{W})}R\Big)

\displaystyle\tilde{R}

\displaystyle=A^{h}_{bal}(\mathbf{W},T_{1-Y}R)+A^{a}_{bal}\Big(\bar{R},T_{(1-Y)^{2}(1+\bar{\mathbf{W}})}\mathbf{W}\Big)+D^{a}_{bal}(\bar{\mathbf{W}},T_{1-\bar{Y}}R).

For above bilinear forms, we compute

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}+T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}\tilde{\mathbf{R}}+\text{cubic and higher terms}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}A^{h}_{bal}(\mathbf{W},R)-2T_{1-\bar{Y}}B^{h}_{bal}(\mathbf{W},R_{\alpha})+2T_{1-\bar{Y}}C^{h}_{bal}(i\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},R)

\displaystyle+T_{(1-\bar{Y})^{2}(1+\mathbf{W})}\partial_{\alpha}A^{a}_{bal}(\bar{\mathbf{W}},R)-T_{(1-\bar{Y})^{2}(1+\mathbf{W})}B^{a}_{bal}(\bar{\mathbf{W}},R_{\alpha})-T_{(1-\bar{Y})^{2}(1+\mathbf{W})}C^{a}_{bal}(i\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},R)

\displaystyle-T_{1-Y}B^{a}_{bal}(\bar{R}_{\alpha},\mathbf{W})+T_{1-Y}C^{a}_{bal}(\bar{R},i\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\mathbf{W})+T_{1-Y}\partial_{\alpha}D^{a}_{bal}(\bar{R},\mathbf{W}),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\tilde{\mathbf{R}}-iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}+\text{cubic and higher terms}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-T_{1-\bar{Y}}A^{h}_{bal}(R_{\alpha},R)-iT_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}C^{h}_{bal}(R,R)

\displaystyle+iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}A^{h}_{bal}(\mathbf{W},\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\mathbf{W})-iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}B^{h}_{bal}(\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle-T_{1-Y}A^{a}_{bal}(\bar{R}_{\alpha},R)-iT_{1-Y}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}C^{a}_{bal}(\bar{R},R)-T_{1-Y}D^{a}_{bal}(\bar{R},R_{\alpha})

\displaystyle+iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}A^{a}_{bal}(\bar{\mathbf{W}},\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\mathbf{W})-iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}B^{a}_{bal}(\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W})-iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}D^{h}_{bal}(\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}).

Here, we denote by
\mathfrak{a}^{h}_{bal}(\xi,\eta)
the symbol of
A^{h}_{bal}(R,T_{1-Y}\mathbf{W})
, and by

\mathfrak{a}^{a}_{bal}(\eta,\zeta)
the symbol of
A^{a}_{bal}\Big(\bar{R},T_{(1-Y)^{2}(1+\bar{\mathbf{W}})}\mathbf{W}\Big)
.
This is similarly done for other balanced bilinear forms of holomorphic or mixed type.
Note that for
\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}
and
\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}
, their symbols are symmetric with respect to
\xi
and
\eta
.
To match the balanced paradifferential source terms of the holomorphic type, paradifferential symbols of the holomorphic type solve the following system:

(4.3)

\begin{cases}(\xi+\eta)\mathfrak{a}^{h}_{bal}-2\eta\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}+2\xi^{4}\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}=(\xi+\eta)\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta),\\
(\xi\mathfrak{a}^{h}_{bal})_{sym}+(\xi+\eta)^{4}\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}=-\frac{1}{2}(\xi+\eta)\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta),\\
(\eta^{4}\mathfrak{a}^{h}_{bal})_{sym}-(\xi+\eta)^{4}\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}=\left(\frac{7}{2}\xi^{4}+\frac{15}{2}\xi^{3}\eta+5\xi^{2}\eta^{2}\right)_{sys}\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta),\end{cases}

where
m_{sym}
stands for the symmetrization of symbol
m
, and
\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta)
is defined in (
A.19
) to select the balanced frequencies.

From the first equation of the system (
4.3
), we get that

(4.4)

\mathfrak{a}^{h}_{bal}(\xi,\eta)=\frac{2\eta}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}-\frac{2\xi^{4}}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}+\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta).

Then we compute two symmetrized symbols

\displaystyle(\eta\mathfrak{a}^{h}_{bal})_{sym}=\frac{2\xi\eta}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}-\frac{\xi^{5}+\eta^{5}}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}+\frac{1}{2}(\xi+\eta)\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta),

\displaystyle(\xi^{4}\mathfrak{a}^{h}_{bal})_{sym}=\frac{\xi^{5}+\eta^{5}}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}-\frac{2\xi^{4}\eta^{4}}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}+\frac{1}{2}(\xi^{4}+\eta^{4})\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta).

Substituting these identities into the second and the third equation of the system (
4.3
),
\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}
and
\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}
solve the system

\begin{cases}\frac{2\xi\eta}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}+\frac{(\xi+\eta)^{5}-(\xi^{5}+\eta^{5})}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}=-(\xi+\eta)\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta),\\
\frac{(\xi^{5}+\eta^{5})-(\xi+\eta)^{5}}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}-\frac{2\xi^{4}\eta^{4}}{\xi+\eta}\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}=\left(\frac{5}{4}\xi^{4}+\frac{15}{4}\xi^{3}\eta+5\xi^{2}\eta^{2}+\frac{15}{4}\xi\eta^{3}+\frac{5}{4}\eta^{4}\right)\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta).\end{cases}

Hence, the solutions of (
4.3
) are

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}^{h}_{bal}=

\displaystyle-\frac{(\xi+\eta)^{2}(25\eta^{6}+100\eta^{5}\xi+200\eta^{4}\xi^{2}+242\eta^{3}\xi^{3}+200\eta^{2}\xi^{4}+100\eta^{2}\xi^{5}+25\xi^{6})}{4\xi\eta(25\xi^{6}+100\xi^{5}\eta+200\xi^{4}\eta^{2}+246\xi^{3}\eta^{3}+200\xi^{2}\eta^{4}+100\xi\eta^{5}+25\eta^{6})}\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta),

\displaystyle\mathfrak{c}^{h}_{bal}=

\displaystyle-\frac{5(\xi+\eta)^{3}(\xi^{2}+\xi\eta+\eta^{2})}{2\xi\eta(25\xi^{6}+100\xi^{5}\eta+200\xi^{4}\eta^{2}+246\xi^{3}\eta^{3}+200\xi^{2}\eta^{4}+100\xi\eta^{5}+25\eta^{6})}\chi_{2}(\xi,\eta),

and
\mathfrak{a}^{h}_{bal}
is given by (
4.4
).

To match the balanced paradifferential source terms of the mixed type, paradifferential symbols of the mixed type solve the following system:

\begin{cases}(\zeta-\eta)\mathfrak{a}^{a}_{bal}-\zeta\mathfrak{b}^{a}_{bal}-\eta^{4}\mathfrak{c}^{a}_{bal}=(\zeta-\eta)\chi_{2}(\eta,\zeta)1_{\zeta<\eta},\\
\eta\mathfrak{b}^{a}_{bal}+\zeta^{4}\mathfrak{c}^{a}_{bal}+(\zeta-\eta)\mathfrak{d}^{a}_{bal}=(\zeta-\eta)\chi_{2}(\eta,\zeta)1_{\zeta<\eta},\\
\eta\mathfrak{a}^{a}_{bal}-(\zeta-\eta)^{4}\mathfrak{c}^{a}_{bal}-\zeta\mathfrak{d}^{a}_{bal}=(\zeta-\eta)\chi_{2}(\eta,\zeta)1_{\zeta<\eta},\\
\zeta^{4}\mathfrak{a}^{a}_{bal}-(\zeta-\eta)^{4}\mathfrak{b}^{a}_{bal}-\eta^{4}\mathfrak{d}^{a}_{bal}=\left(\frac{3}{2}\zeta^{4}-\frac{5}{2}\zeta^{3}\eta+\frac{5}{2}\zeta\eta^{3}-\frac{3}{2}\eta^{4}\right)\chi_{2}(\eta,\zeta)1_{\zeta<\eta}.\end{cases}

The solutions of the above system are given by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{a}^{a}_{bal}=

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta(-12\eta^{6}+51\eta^{5}\zeta-121\eta^{4}\zeta^{2}+147\eta^{3}\zeta^{3}-95\eta^{2}\zeta^{4}+25\eta\zeta^{5}+5\zeta^{6})}{2\eta(4\eta^{6}-12\eta^{5}\zeta+37\eta^{4}\zeta-54\eta^{3}\zeta^{3}+75\eta^{2}\zeta^{4}-50\eta\zeta^{5}+25\zeta^{6})}\chi_{2}(\eta,\zeta)1_{\zeta<\eta},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}^{a}_{bal}=

\displaystyle\frac{2\eta^{7}-13\eta^{6}\zeta+22\eta^{5}\zeta^{2}-28\eta^{4}\zeta^{3}-8\eta^{3}\zeta^{4}+30\eta^{2}\zeta^{5}-30\eta\zeta^{6}+5\zeta^{7}}{2\eta(4\eta^{6}-12\eta^{5}\zeta+37\eta^{4}\zeta-54\eta^{3}\zeta^{3}+75\eta^{2}\zeta^{4}-50\eta\zeta^{5}+25\zeta^{6})}\chi_{2}(\eta,\zeta)1_{\zeta<\eta},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{c}^{a}_{bal}=

\displaystyle\frac{4\eta^{4}-11\eta^{2}\zeta+24\eta^{2}\zeta^{2}-16\eta\zeta^{3}+9\zeta^{4}}{4\eta^{6}-12\eta^{5}\zeta+37\eta^{4}\zeta-54\eta^{3}\zeta^{3}+75\eta^{2}\zeta^{4}-50\eta\zeta^{5}+25\zeta^{6}}\chi_{2}(\eta,\zeta)1_{\zeta<\eta},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{d}^{a}_{bal}=

\displaystyle-\frac{10\eta^{7}-35\eta^{6}\zeta+85\eta^{5}\zeta^{2}-125\eta^{4}\zeta^{3}+133\eta^{3}\zeta^{4}-109\eta^{2}\zeta^{5}+59\eta\zeta^{6}-18\zeta^{7}}{2(4\eta^{6}-12\eta^{5}\zeta+37\eta^{4}\zeta-54\eta^{3}\zeta^{3}+75\eta^{2}\zeta^{4}-50\eta\zeta^{5}+25\zeta^{6})}\chi_{2}(\eta,\zeta)1_{\zeta<\eta}.

The resulting expressions for the symbols confirm that the normal form corrections
(\tilde{\mathbf{W}},\tilde{R})
satisfy the estimate:

\|(\tilde{\mathbf{W}},\tilde{R})\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}}\lesssim\mathcal{A}_{0}\|(\mathbf{W},R)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}}.

Moreover,
(\tilde{\mathbf{W}},\tilde{R})
solve equations

\displaystyle T_{D_{t}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}+T_{b_{\alpha}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}+\partial_{\alpha}T_{1-\bar{Y}}T_{1+\mathbf{W}}\tilde{R}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}\Pi(\mathbf{W},R)+T_{1-Y}\partial_{\alpha}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{R},\mathbf{W})+T_{(1-\bar{Y})^{2}(1+\mathbf{W})}\partial_{\alpha}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},R)+G,

and

\displaystyle T_{D_{t}}\tilde{R}+T_{b_{\alpha}}\tilde{R}-iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}+10iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}

\displaystyle-\frac{65}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{4}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}-10iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{1-\bar{Y}}\Pi(R_{\alpha},R)+T_{1-Y}{\mathbf{P}}\partial_{\alpha}\Pi(\bar{R},R)+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})+5iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}\Pi(\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}},\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})+\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W}_{\alpha})-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}{\mathbf{P}}\Pi(\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\mathbf{W})+K.

Thus, we define
(\mathbf{W}_{NF},R_{NF}):=(\mathbf{W},R)+(\tilde{\mathbf{W}},\tilde{R})
, which satisfy the norm equivalence

(4.5)

\|(\mathbf{W}_{NF}-\mathbf{W},R_{NF}-R)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}}\lesssim{\mathcal{A}_{0}}\|(\mathbf{W},R)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}},\quad s>0.

Moreover, the pair
(\mathbf{W}_{NF},R_{NF})
solves the following linear paradifferential system with perturbative source terms.

(4.6)

\begin{cases}&T_{D_{t}}\hat{w}+T_{b_{\alpha}}\hat{w}+\partial_{\alpha}T_{1-\bar{Y}}T_{1+\mathbf{W}}\hat{r}=G,\\
&T_{D_{t}}\hat{r}+T_{b_{\alpha}}\hat{r}-iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\hat{w}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\hat{w}\\
&+\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\hat{w}+10iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}\\
&-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}\\
&-\frac{65}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{4}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-10iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}=K.\end{cases}

4.3.
Further reduction to the paradifferential homogeneous linearized flow

Having reduced the differentiated hydroelastic waves (
1.7
) to the system (
4.6
), we now proceed to the derivation of the modified energy estimate for (
4.6
).
Rather than analyzing (
4.6
) directly, we reduce it to the paradifferential homogeneous linearized flow (
3.1
) with perturbative source terms, and obtain the
{\mathcal{H}}^{s}
modified energy estimate for (
3.1
).

Let the pair
(\hat{w},\hat{r})
be a solution of the system (
4.6
), we consider

(4.7)

(w,r):=(\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\hat{w},\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}T_{1+\mathbf{W}}\hat{r}-\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}T_{R_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\hat{w}),

so that
(w_{\alpha},r_{\alpha})=(\hat{w},T_{1+\mathbf{W}}\hat{r}-T_{R_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\hat{w})
, and

(4.8)

\|(w_{\alpha},r_{\alpha})-(\hat{w},\hat{r})\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}}\lesssim{\mathcal{A}_{0}}\|(\hat{w},\hat{r})\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}},\quad\forall s\in\mathbb{R}.

We then differentiate the left-hand side of the paradifferential flow (
3.1
), and get for the first equation

\partial_{\alpha}(T_{D_{t}}w+T_{1-\bar{Y}}r_{\alpha}+T_{(1-\bar{Y})R_{\alpha}}w)=T_{D_{t}}\hat{w}+T_{b_{\alpha}}\hat{w}+\partial_{\alpha}T_{1-\bar{Y}}T_{1+\mathbf{W}}\hat{r}=\tilde{G}.

As for the second equation of (
3.1
), we again take the
\alpha
-derivative and use the material derivative of
\mathbf{W}
and
R
to write

\displaystyle\quad\partial_{\alpha}(T_{D_{t}}r-i\mathcal{L}_{para}w)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{D_{t}}(T_{1+\mathbf{W}}\hat{r}-T_{R_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\hat{w})+T_{b_{\alpha}}(T_{1+\mathbf{W}}\hat{r}-T_{R_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\hat{w})-i\partial_{\alpha}T_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\hat{w}

\displaystyle+5i\partial_{\alpha}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}+i\partial_{\alpha}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}+5i\partial_{\alpha}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle-i\partial_{\alpha}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}-15i\partial_{\alpha}T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}+\frac{5}{2}i\partial_{\alpha}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}i\partial_{\alpha}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}+i\partial_{\alpha}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\hat{w}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{1+\mathbf{W}}(T_{D_{t}}+T_{b_{\alpha}})\hat{r}-T_{R_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}(T_{D_{t}}+T_{b_{\alpha}})\hat{w}+T_{D_{t}\mathbf{W}}\hat{r}-T_{\partial_{\alpha}D_{t}R}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\hat{w}

\displaystyle-iT_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\hat{w}+\frac{5}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\hat{w}+\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\hat{w}+5iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\hat{w}

\displaystyle+5iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{35}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{15}{2}iT_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\hat{w}+iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+5iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}+5iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}-iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle-15iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{5}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}+\frac{5}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}+iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}+iT_{((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W})_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\hat{w}+\tilde{K}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{R_{\alpha}}T_{1-\bar{Y}}T_{1+\mathbf{W}}\hat{r}-T_{(1+\mathbf{W})(1-\bar{Y})R_{\alpha}-(1+\mathbf{W})M}\hat{r}+iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\hat{w}

\displaystyle-\frac{15}{2}iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\hat{w}-\frac{5}{2}iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\hat{w}-10iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{15}{2}iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{3}}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}+\frac{5}{2}iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}-\frac{7}{2}iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)}T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}+\frac{65}{2}iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1-Y)^{4}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}+10iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{2}iT_{1+\mathbf{W}}T_{J^{-\frac{7}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-iT_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\hat{w}+\frac{15}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\hat{w}+\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\hat{w}

\displaystyle+10iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{65}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}-10iT_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}+iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\hat{w}

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\hat{w}_{\alpha\alpha}+5iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}-i\frac{5}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}+\frac{5}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{7}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\hat{w}-\frac{3}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\hat{w}+T_{(i(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}-D_{t}R)_{\alpha}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\hat{w}+\tilde{K}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{K},

where
(\tilde{G},\tilde{K})
belong to perturbative terms that satisfy

\|(\tilde{G},\tilde{K})\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}}\lesssim_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}{\mathcal{A}_{\sharp,\frac{7}{4}}^{2}}\|(w,r)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s+1}}.

Therefore, we have shown the following result that connects variables
(w,r)
and
(\hat{w},\hat{r})
.

Proposition 4.1
.

Given
(\hat{w},\hat{r})
that solve the system of equation (
4.6
), then
(w,r)
defined in (
4.7
)
solve the system of paradifferential equation (
3.1
) for any
s\in\mathbb{R}
.
Moreover, the transformation is invertible in the sense of (
4.8
).

Hence, according to the previous Proposition, the
{\mathcal{H}}^{s+1}
modified energy estimate for (
3.1
) implies the
{\mathcal{H}}^{s}
modified energy estimate of (
4.6
) for
s>-1
.

4.4.
{\mathcal{H}}^{s}
energy estimate of the homogeneous paradifferential system

In this section, we generalize the result in Proposition
3.2
to
s\geq 0
.
In other words, we prove the following modified energy estimate of (
3.1
) for
s\geq 0
.

Proposition 4.2
.

Assume that
\mathcal{A}_{0}\lesssim 1
and
\mathcal{A}_{\sharp,\frac{7}{4}}\in L^{2}_{t}([0,T])
for some time
T>0
, then if
(w,r)
solve the homogeneous paradifferential system (
3.1
) on
[0,T]
, there exists an energy functional
E^{s,para}_{lin}(w,r)
such that on
[0,T]
for
s\geq 0
:

(1)

Norm equivalence:

E^{s,para}_{lin}(w,r)=(1+O({\mathcal{A}_{0}}))\|(w,r)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}}^{2}.

(2)

The time derivative of
E^{s,para}_{lin}(w,r)
is bounded by

\frac{d}{dt}E^{s,para}_{lin}(w,r)\lesssim_{{\mathcal{A}_{0}}}{\mathcal{A}_{\sharp,\frac{7}{4}}^{2}}\|(w,r)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}}^{2}.

We apply the operator
\langle D\rangle^{s}
to the homogeneous paradifferential system (
3.1
).
Then
(w^{s},r^{s}):=\langle D\rangle^{s}(w,r)
solve the paradifferential equations

(4.9)

\begin{cases}T_{D_{t}}w^{s}+T_{1-\bar{Y}}r_{\alpha}^{s}+T_{T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}}w^{s}=\mathcal{G}^{s}_{0}+G_{s},\\
T_{D_{t}}r^{s}-i\mathcal{L}_{para}w^{s}=\mathcal{K}^{s}_{0}+K_{s},\end{cases}

where the
(G_{s},K_{s})
are perturbative source terms that satisfy

(4.10)

\|(G_{s},K_{s})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}\lesssim_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}{\mathcal{A}_{\sharp,\frac{7}{4}}^{2}}\|(w,r)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}},

and source terms
(\mathcal{G}^{s}_{0},\mathcal{K}^{s}_{0})
are given by the sum of paradifferential commutator terms:

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{s}_{0}=

\displaystyle L(b_{\alpha},w^{s})-L(\bar{Y}_{\alpha},r^{s})+L((T_{1-\bar{Y}}\bar{R}_{\alpha})_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}w^{s}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}^{s}_{0}=

\displaystyle L(b_{\alpha},r^{s})-iL([(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}]_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{3}w^{s})+5iL([(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}]_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}w^{s})

\displaystyle+iL((J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha})_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}w^{s})+5iL([(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}]_{\alpha},w^{s}_{\alpha})-iL((J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha})_{\alpha},w^{s}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle-15iL([(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}]_{\alpha},w^{s}_{\alpha})+\frac{5}{2}iL([(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\mathbf{W}]_{\alpha},w^{s})

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}iL([J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\bar{\mathbf{W}}]_{\alpha},w^{s})+iL([(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\mathbf{W}]_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}w^{s}).

Here,
L
denotes the zero order paradifferential commutator

(4.11)

L(f_{\alpha},u)=-[\langle D\rangle^{s},T_{f}]\partial_{\alpha}\langle D\rangle^{-s}u\approx-sT_{f_{\alpha}}u+\text{lower order terms}.

To have a clear view of
(\mathcal{G}^{s}_{0},\mathcal{K}^{s}_{0})
, we rewrite them as the sum of non-perturbative terms plus perturbative source terms
(G_{s},K_{s})
.

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{s}_{0}

\displaystyle=L(T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}+T_{1-Y}\bar{R}_{\alpha},w^{s})-L((1-\bar{Y})^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},r^{s})+L(T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}w^{s})+G_{s},

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}^{s}_{0}

\displaystyle=L(T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}+T_{1-Y}\bar{R}_{\alpha},r^{s})+\frac{5}{2}iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{3}w^{s})+\frac{3}{2}iL(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{3}w^{s})

\displaystyle+5iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}w^{s})+iL(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}w^{s})-\frac{35}{2}iL((1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}w^{s})

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}iL((1-\bar{Y})J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}w^{s})-10iL((1-Y)J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}w^{s})

\displaystyle+5iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\mathbf{W},w^{s}_{\alpha})-iL(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\bar{\mathbf{W}},w^{s}_{\alpha})+\frac{5}{2}iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\mathbf{W},w^{s})

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}iL(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\bar{\mathbf{W}},w^{s})+iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{5}\mathbf{W},\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}w^{s})+K_{s}.

We further apply the paradifferential conjugation
T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}
to the system (
4.13
), and set
(\tilde{w}^{s},\tilde{r}^{s}):=T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}(w^{s},r^{s})=T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}\langle D\rangle^{s}(w,r)
.
One can think of (
3.1
) as the quasilinear dispersive equation

\Big(\partial_{t}+iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{4}}}|D|^{\frac{5}{2}}\Big)w=\text{lower order nonlinear terms}.

Since the leading part of the elliptic operator
\Big(T_{J^{-\frac{5}{4}}}|D|^{\frac{5}{2}}\Big)^{\frac{2s}{5}}\approx T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}|D|^{s}
, the paradifferential conjugation
T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}
is added to cancel some of the lower order non-perturbative terms.

We first compute each derivatives of
w^{s}
, writing them as the sum of derivatives of
\tilde{w}_{s}
.

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}w^{s}=\partial_{\alpha}T_{J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+K_{s}=T_{\frac{s}{2}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+T_{\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+K_{s},

\displaystyle\partial^{2}_{\alpha}w^{s}=\partial^{2}_{\alpha}T_{J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+K_{s}=T_{J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+2T_{\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+T_{\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+K_{s},

\displaystyle\partial^{3}_{\alpha}w^{s}=\partial^{3}_{\alpha}T_{J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+K_{s}=T_{J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}+3T_{\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+3T_{\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+K_{s},

\displaystyle\partial^{4}_{\alpha}w^{s}=\partial^{4}_{\alpha}T_{J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+K_{s}=T_{J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}+4T_{\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}+6T_{\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+4T_{\partial^{3}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+T_{\partial^{4}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+K_{s}.

One can also check that

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{s}{2}J^{\frac{s}{2}}((1-Y)\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}+(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}),

\displaystyle\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{s}{2}J^{\frac{s}{2}}((1-Y)\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+(1-\bar{Y})\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha})+\frac{s}{2}\left(\frac{s}{2}-1\right)J^{\frac{s}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{s^{2}}{2}J^{\frac{s}{2}-1}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}+\frac{s}{2}\left(\frac{s}{2}-1\right)J^{\frac{s}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}^{2}_{\alpha},

\displaystyle\partial^{3}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{s}{2}J^{\frac{s}{2}}((1-Y)\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+(1-\bar{Y})\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}})+\frac{3s}{2}\left(\frac{s}{2}-1\right)J^{\frac{s}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{3s^{2}}{4}J^{\frac{s}{2}-1}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{3s^{2}}{4}J^{\frac{s}{2}-1}(1-\bar{Y})\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}+\frac{3s}{2}\left(\frac{s}{2}-1\right)J^{\frac{s}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+\text{cubic terms with at most one derivative on $\mathbf{W}$ or $\bar{\mathbf{W}}$},

\displaystyle\partial^{4}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}=

\displaystyle\frac{s}{2}J^{\frac{s}{2}}((1-Y)\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+(1-\bar{Y})\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}})+\left(s^{2}-2s\right)J^{\frac{s}{2}}(1-Y)^{2}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}

\displaystyle+s^{2}J^{\frac{s}{2}-1}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}+s^{2}J^{\frac{s}{2}-1}(1-\bar{Y})\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}+(s^{2}-2s)J^{\frac{s}{2}}(1-\bar{Y})^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}

\displaystyle+\text{cubic terms with at most two derivatives on $\mathbf{W}$ or $\bar{\mathbf{W}}$}.

For non-elastic terms, following Section 5 in
[
3
]
, we have

\displaystyle T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}T_{D_{t}}w^{s}

\displaystyle=T_{D_{t}}\tilde{w}^{s}-\frac{s}{2}T_{b_{\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}+G_{s},

\displaystyle T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}T_{D_{t}}r^{s}

\displaystyle=T_{D_{t}}\tilde{r}^{s}-\frac{s}{2}T_{b_{\alpha}}\tilde{r}^{s}+K_{s},

\displaystyle T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}r^{s}

\displaystyle=T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}\tilde{r}^{s}+\frac{s}{2}(T_{(1-\bar{Y})}T_{(1+\mathbf{W})Y_{\alpha}}+T_{\bar{Y}_{\alpha}})\tilde{r}^{s}+G_{s}

\displaystyle=T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}\tilde{r}^{s}+\frac{s}{2}T_{J^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\tilde{r}^{s}+\frac{s}{2}T_{(1-\bar{Y})^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\tilde{r}^{s}+G_{s},

\displaystyle T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}T_{(1-\bar{Y})R_{\alpha}}w^{s}

\displaystyle=T_{(1-\bar{Y})R_{\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}+G_{s}.

As for linearized elastic terms,

\displaystyle T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}\mathcal{L}_{para}w^{s}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\tilde{w}^{s}+4T_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}+6T_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+4T_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+T_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}-5T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}

\displaystyle-15T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}-15T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}-5T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}

\displaystyle-T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}-3T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}-T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}

\displaystyle-5T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}-10T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}-5T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}

\displaystyle+T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+2T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+15T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+30T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+15T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}-\frac{5}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}

\displaystyle-T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}+K_{s}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\tilde{w}^{s}+2sT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}+2sT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}+3sT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+3sT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+3s\left(\frac{s}{2}-1\right)T_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+3s^{2}T_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+3s\left(\frac{s}{2}-1\right)T_{(1-\bar{Y})J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+2sT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+2sT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{s}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}+\frac{s}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{w}^{s}-5T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}-\frac{15}{2}sT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle-\frac{15}{2}sT_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}-T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}-\frac{3s}{2}T_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{3s}{2}T_{(1-\bar{Y})J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle-5T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+15T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}-\frac{5}{2}T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}T_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}-T_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}+K_{s}.

For the action of
T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}
to source terms
(\mathcal{G}^{s}_{0},\mathcal{K}^{s}_{0})
, we compute

\displaystyle T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}\mathcal{G}^{s}_{0}=

\displaystyle L(b_{\alpha},\tilde{w}^{s})-L(\bar{Y}_{\alpha},\tilde{r}^{s})+L((T_{1-\bar{Y}}\bar{R}_{\alpha})_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\tilde{w}^{s}),

\displaystyle T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}}\mathcal{K}^{s}_{0}=

\displaystyle L(b_{\alpha},\tilde{r}^{s})-iL([(1-Y)J^{-\frac{3}{2}}]_{\alpha},\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}+3T_{J^{-\frac{s}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}J^{\frac{s}{2}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha})+5iL([(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}]_{\alpha},\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha})

\displaystyle+iL((J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha})_{\alpha},\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha})+5iL([(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha}]_{\alpha},\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha})-iL((J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha})_{\alpha},\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle-15iL([(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2}]_{\alpha},\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha})+\frac{5}{2}iL([(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\mathbf{W}]_{\alpha},\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha})

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}iL([J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\bar{\mathbf{W}}]_{\alpha},\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha})+iL([(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\mathbf{W}]_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\tilde{w}^{s})+K_{s}.

Collecting all the terms above and simplifying, we get that
(\tilde{w}^{s},\tilde{r}^{s})
solve the system of paradifferential equations

(4.12)

\begin{cases}T_{D_{t}}\tilde{w}^{s}+T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}\tilde{r}^{s}+T_{T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}=\mathcal{G}^{s}_{1}+G_{s},\\
T_{D_{t}}\tilde{r}^{s}-i\mathcal{L}_{para}\tilde{w}^{s}=\mathcal{K}^{s}_{1}+K_{s},\end{cases}

where non-perturbative source terms
(\mathcal{G}^{s}_{1},\mathcal{K}^{s}_{1})
are given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{s}_{1}=

\displaystyle L(T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}+T_{1-Y}\bar{R}_{\alpha},\tilde{w}^{s})-L((1-\bar{Y})^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\tilde{r}^{s})+L(T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\tilde{w}^{s})

\displaystyle+\frac{s}{2}T_{T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}+\frac{s}{2}T_{T_{1-Y}\bar{R}_{\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}-\frac{s}{2}T_{J^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\tilde{r}^{s}-\frac{s}{2}T_{(1-\bar{Y})^{2}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\tilde{r}^{s}+G_{s},

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}^{s}_{1}=

\displaystyle L(T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}+T_{1-Y}\bar{R}_{\alpha},\tilde{r}^{s})+2siT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}+2siT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\tilde{w}^{s}

\displaystyle+3siT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+3siT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{2}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+3s\left(\frac{s}{2}-\frac{7}{2}\right)iT_{(1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}

\displaystyle+3s(s-3)iT_{(1-Y)J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+3s\left(\frac{s}{2}-\frac{3}{2}\right)iT_{(1-\bar{Y})J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha\alpha}+2siT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+2siT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{3}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha}+\frac{s}{2}iT_{(1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\mathbf{W}}\tilde{w}^{s}+\frac{s}{2}iT_{J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial^{4}_{\alpha}\bar{\mathbf{W}}}\tilde{w}^{s}+\frac{s}{2}T_{T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}}\tilde{r}^{s}

\displaystyle+\frac{s}{2}T_{T_{1-Y}\bar{R}_{\alpha}}\tilde{r}^{s}+\frac{5}{2}iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\tilde{w}^{s})+\frac{15}{4}siL((1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}^{2}_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\tilde{w}^{s})

\displaystyle+6siL((1-Y)J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\tilde{w}^{s})+\frac{3}{2}iL(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\tilde{w}^{s})+\frac{9}{4}siL((1-\bar{Y})J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}^{2}_{\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\tilde{w}^{s})

\displaystyle+5iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\tilde{w}^{s})+iL(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha\alpha},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\tilde{w}^{s})-\frac{35}{2}iL((1-Y)^{3}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}^{2},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\tilde{w}^{s})

\displaystyle-\frac{5}{2}iL((1-\bar{Y})J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\mathbf{W}}_{\alpha}^{2},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}w^{s})-10iL((1-Y)J^{-\frac{5}{2}}|\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}|^{2},\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\tilde{w}^{s})

\displaystyle+5iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\mathbf{W},\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha})-iL(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\tilde{w}^{s}_{\alpha})+\frac{5}{2}iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\mathbf{W},\tilde{w}^{s})

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}iL(J^{-\frac{5}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\bar{\mathbf{W}},\tilde{w}^{s})+iL((1-Y)^{2}J^{-\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{\alpha}^{5}\mathbf{W},\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\tilde{w}^{s})+K_{s}.

Since
(\mathcal{G}^{s}_{1},\mathcal{K}^{s}_{1})
still have non-perturbative components, we cannot apply Proposition
3.2
to the system (
4.9
) directly to obtain Proposition
4.2
.
We will construct normal form variables
(w_{NF}^{s},r_{NF}^{s})
as the sum of
(\tilde{w}^{s},\tilde{r}^{s})
, the quadratic normal form corrections
(w^{s}_{1},r^{s}_{1})
, and the cubic normal form corrections
(w^{s}_{2},r^{s}_{2})
satisfying

\|(w^{s}_{1},r^{s}_{1})\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{0}}\lesssim{\mathcal{A}_{0}}\|(w,r)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}},\quad\|(w^{s}_{2},r^{s}_{2})\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{0}}\lesssim\mathcal{A}^{2}_{0}\|(w,r)\|_{{\mathcal{H}}^{s}}.

Moreover,
(w_{NF}^{s},r_{NF}^{s})
solve the system

(4.13)

\begin{cases}T_{D_{t}}w_{NF}^{s}+T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}r_{NF}^{s}+T_{T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}}w_{NF}^{s}=\mathcal{G}^{s,res}_{2}+G_{s},\\
T_{D_{t}}r_{NF}^{s}-i\mathcal{L}_{para}w_{NF}^{s}=\mathcal{K}^{s,res}_{2}+K_{s},\end{cases}

where
(\mathcal{G}^{s,res}_{2},\mathcal{K}^{s,res}_{2})
are non-perturbative cubic terms that may have resonances.
Proposition
3.2
can then be applied to the paradifferential system (
4.13
).
The modified energy

(4.14)

E^{s,para}_{lin}(w,r):=E^{0,para}_{lin}(w_{NF}^{s},r_{NF}^{s})+E^{s}_{4,cor}(\tilde{w}^{s},\tilde{r}^{s})

is the final energy required for Proposition
4.2
,
where the energy
E^{0,para}_{lin}(w,r)
is the modified energy constructed in Proposition
3.2
, and
E^{s}_{4,cor}
is a quartic modified energy.

In the rest of this section, we will construct the quadratic normal form corrections
(w^{s}_{1},r^{s}_{1})
, and the cubic normal form corrections
(w^{s}_{2},r^{s}_{2})
and the quartic modified energy
E^{s}_{4,cor}(\tilde{w}^{s},\tilde{r}^{s})
.

4.4.1.
Construction of quadratic normal form corrections
(w^{s}_{1},r^{s}_{1})

We will first construct quadratic normal form corrections
(w^{s}_{1},r^{s}_{1})
such that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}w^{s}_{1}+T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}r^{s}_{1}+\text{cubic and higher terms}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{\alpha}L(R_{\alpha},T_{1-\bar{Y}}\partial_{\alpha}^{-1}\tilde{w}^{s})-\frac{s}{2}T_{T_{1-\bar{Y}}R_{\alpha}}\tilde{w}^{s}+\frac{s}{2}T_{J^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{\alpha}}\tilde{r}^{s}
